2023 年 行 業 經 營 環 境 出 現 較 大 變 化，行 業 產 能   2023 is a challenging year for the industry with significant changes in
增速較快，而下游需求增速卻未達到預期，導致                    the operating environment. Due to the rapid growth in production
多個主要氟硅化工產品出現價格明顯下滑情況，                    capacity and the weaker-than-expected downstream demand, the
行業市場不容樂觀。面對下行的市場，本集團發                    prices of various major fluorosilicon chemical products recorded steep
揮自身的經營優勢，迅速改變應對策略，在嚴峻                    declines, resulting in a less optimistic market view. Facing such market
的市場變化中穩健經營，使得本集團在行業中仍                    downturn, the Group fully utilized its operating advantage and quickly
保持較高競爭力。                                 adjusted its business strategy to cope with the changes. As a result,
                                         amid severe market volatility, the Group maintained stable operation
                                         and strong competitiveness in the industry.

   本 回 顧 期 內，由 於 市 場 變 化 較 大，氟 硅 化          During the period under review, due to significant market
   工 多 個 產 品 價 格 明 顯 下 滑，利 潤 空 間 受 到        changes the prices of various fluorosilicon chemical products
   壓 縮。本 集 團 管 理 層 針 對 此 變 化 迅 速 制 定        recorded a substantial decline, resulting in a profit margin
   經 營 策 略，穩 健 經 營，苦 練「內 功」，節 約             squeeze. In response to these changes, the Group ’s
   挖 潛，並 憑 藉 本 集 團 龐 大 的 規 模、先 進 的          management quickly formulated business strategies to maintain
   技 術、完 善 的 產 業 鏈、優 秀 的 管 理 等 多 方          prudent operations, reinforce “internal strength”, reduce costs
   面 優 勢，幫 助 本 集 團 在 行 業 中 保 持 較 高 競        and explore potential developments. The Group’s economies of
   爭 力。年 內，本 集 團 生 產 運 行 平 穩，在 嚴            scale, advanced technology, well-established production chain
   峻 的 市 場 壓 力 下，部 分 主 要 產 品 產 銷 量 仍        and outstanding management helped the Group maintain
   能 實 現 一 定 的 增 長，市 場 佔 有 率 進 一 步 提        strong competitiveness in the industry. During the year, despite
   升。雖 然 回 顧 期 內 本 集 團 受 市 場 影 響 導 致        severe market pressure, the Group’s production remained
   收 入 與 利 潤 皆 有 較 大 下 滑，但 本 集 團 相 信        stable. Some major products achieved growth in both
  「行穩致遠」的經營策略能夠幫助本集團順                       production and sales volume to help uplift the Group’s market
   利 度 過 行 業 低 谷 期，為 未 來 行 業 復 蘇 帶 來        share. During the period under review, although the Group
   的機會做好充足準備。                               recorded a significant decline on revenue and profit due to
                                            negative market factors, the Group’s business strategy of

3.   繼續以安全環保生產為最大競爭力                     3.   Ongoing safe and environmentally friendly production as
                                              the greatest competitive edge

     本集團管理層深知安全環保對於生產的重                       The Group’s management recognises the importance of safety
     要 性，要 成 為「行 穩 致 遠」的 企 業，必 須 不            and environmental protection in production process. To be a
     斷 提 升 安 全 環 保 生 產 水 平。本 集 團 今 後 將        “steady progress” company, the ongoing improvement on the
     繼續以「零傷害、零事故、零泄漏」為目標，                     standard of safety and environmental protection production is
     全面落實生產安全責任制，加強安全投入、                      essential. To uphold the vision of “zero injury, zero accident,
     安全培訓、基礎管理、應急救援。另外，本                      and zero leakage”, the Group will fully enforce the production
     集團還將進一步全面推動生產過程安全管                       safety responsibility system. We will strengthen investment,
     理 和 智 能 數 字 化 行 動，實 現 本 質 安 全，減          training, basic management, and emergency rescue measures
     少 生 產 環 節 的 人 為 干 預，從 而 降 低 風 險，         with regard to safeness. In addition, the Group will take a
     同時還能提升運行效率、減少環節浪費。                       comprehensive approach to further promote safety
                                              management and intelligent digitalisation within our production
                                              processes to achieve essential safety. This strategy aims to lower
                                              human intervention in the production process, thereby reducing
                                              risks, enhancing operating efficiency, and minimising processing
                                              wastes.

     量，建 立 數 據 庫，制 定 一 流 質 量 標 準；工            establish database and set up top quality standard. In terms of

5.   優化採購標準，進一步降本增效                      5.   Optimizing procurement standards for further cost
                                              reduction and efficiency improvement

     面 對 未 來 的 經 營 挑 戰，本 集 團 已 將 採 購 作        In view of the operating challenges ahead, the Group has
     為 降 本 增 效 的 主 要 方 向。本 集 團 將 優 化 原        identified procurement as a key direction for cost reduction and
     材 料 採 購 標 準，建 立 更 加 嚴 格 的 供 應 商 准        efficiency improvement. The Group will optimize standards for
     入 標 準，嚴 肅 執 行 供 應 商 禁 止 性 規 定，把          the procurement of raw materials to adopt stricter approval
     供應商情況以及對供應商的監管形成數據                       standards for suppliers, strictly implement supplier prohibitive
     庫。對 於 採 購 的 執 行，集 團 審 計 稽 核 部 門          provisions, and establish a database on supplier status and
     將 進 行 全 面 監 督，嚴 格 按 規 定 提 報、招 投          supervision. The Group’s Audit Department will comprehensively
     標、制定合同、執行合同。另外，本集團將                      supervise the implementation of procurement, and strictly
     堅 決 消 除 不 合 格 中 間 商 貿 易 商，加 大 廠 家        follow the regulations on document submission, bidding,
     直供比例。                                    contract preparation and execution. In addition, the Group will
                                              resolutely remove unqualified intermediary traders and increase
                                              the proportion of direct supply from manufacturers.

2023 年，氟硅化工行業經歷了風霜雨雪，本集團                 In 2023, the fluorosilicon chemical industry witnessed various
憑 藉 行 業 37 年 的 經 營 經 驗，經 受 住 了 行 業 市 場   difficulties, and the Group managed to survive these changes based
變 化 的 考 驗。2024 年 將 是 充 滿 著 機 遇 和 挑 戰 的   on its 37 years of operating experience in the industry. 2024 will be a
一年，我們將堅持正確的經營理念，保持緊迫感、                   year full of opportunities and challenges. We will adhere to sound
危機感、責任感，專注氟硅化工領域，開拓進取，                   business principles, maintain a sense of urgency, crisis and
不斷奮鬥，努力創造可觀的價值，回報投資者長                    responsibility; focus on the field of fluorosilicon chemical, and work

董事會採納的股息政策旨在讓本公司於建議股息                       The dividend policy adopted by the Board is intended for the
時，維持足夠現金儲備以應付其營運及資金需求，                      Company to maintain adequate cash reserves to meet its operational
為未來增長提供資金，以及如有派息時，提高股                       and capital requirements, fund its future growth and enhance
東價值，作出派息須符合適用法律、規則及規例                       shareholder value if and when dividends are recommended subject to
以及本公司的章程。根據股息政策，董事會宣派                       applicable laws, rules and regulations and the articles of association of
任何股息視乎以下因素，包括本集團的實際及預                       the Company. According to the dividend policy, the declaration of
期財務表現、本集團的可供分派儲備、本集團的                       any dividends will depend on the following factors, including actual
營運資金及資本開支需求、本集團的流動資金狀                       and expected financial performance of the Group, the distributable
況、整體經濟狀況及董事會認為相關的任何其他                       reserves of the Group, the working capital and capital expenditure
因素。董事會在推薦建議截至 2023 年 12 月 31 日              requirements of the Group, the liquidity position of the Group, the
止年度的末期股息時已考慮相關因素。                           overall economic situation, and any other factors that the Board
                                            considers relevant. The Board has considered the relevant factors
                                            when recommending the final dividends for the year ended 31
                                            December 2023.

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE REPORTS


東岳集團有限公司（「本公司」）董事會（「董事會」）                 The board of Directors (the “Board”) of Dongyue Group Limited (the
肩負為本公司股東（「股東」）創造價值及帶來最大                   “Company”) recognizes their mission of creating values and
回報的使命，同時履行其企業責任。為此，本公                     maximizing returns to the shareholders of the Company (the
司矢志提升及保持最高水準的企業管治。                        “Shareholders”), while at the same time fulfilling their corporate
                                          responsibilities. To this end, we strive to promote and uphold the
                                          highest standard of corporate governance.

董事會已檢討本公司的企業管治常規，並採納及                     The Board has reviewed the corporate governance practices of the
改進各項程序及文書處理方式，詳情載於本報告。                    Company with the adoption and improvement of the various
據 董 事 所 盡 知 及 盡 信，本 公 司 於 截 至 2023 年 12   procedures and documentation which are detailed in this report. To
月 31 日止年度及截至本報告日期採納並一直遵                   the best knowledge and belief of the Directors, the Company has
守 上 市 規 則 附 錄 十 四 所 載 的 企 業 管 治 守 則（「守    adopted and complied with the code provisions of the Corporate
則」）的守則條文，惟關於主席與行政總裁的角色                    Governance Code contained in Appendix 14 of the Listing Rules (the
偏離守則除外，有關詳情披露於本報告下文相關                     “Code”) during the year ended 31 December 2023 and up to the
段落。                                       date of this report, except for deviation regarding the roles of
                                          chairman and chief executive officer, the details of which have been
                                          disclosed in the relevant paragraphs below in this report.

董事會                                       The Board
董事會負責領導和監控本公司及其附屬公司（統                     The Board is responsible for leadership and control of the Company
稱「本集團」）及監察本集團的業務、策略性方針                    and its subsidiaries (together the “Group”), and oversees the Group’s
和表現。董事會轉授權力和責任予本集團管理人                     businesses, strategic direction and performance. The Board delegates
員，以管理本集團。此外，董事會亦已轉授若干                     the authority and responsibility to the Group’s management for the
責任予董事會轄下的委員會。本公司定期對董事                     management of the Group. In addition, the Board has also delegated
會的組成進行檢討，以確保董事會成員在專業、                     various responsibilities to the Board’s Committees. The composition of
技能及經驗方面實現良好平衡，符合本集團業務                     the Board is reviewed regularly to ensure that it has a good balance of
的要求。                                      expertise, skills and experience which can meet the requirements of

董事的委任及重選                                  Appointment and re-election of Directors
新董事的委任及其他相關事項已委派予本公司提                     The appointment of new Director(s) and other related matters have
名委員會負責。提名委員會就相關事項（包括本                     been delegated to the Company’s Nomination Committee, which
公司董事的委任及重新委任）向董事會提供建議。                    would make recommendations to the Board on relevant matters
董事會認為，預期董事應具備有關範疇的專業知                     including the appointment or reappointment of the Directors of the
識，以為本公司作出貢獻，且有足夠時間參與本                     Company. The Board considers that the Director(s) is expected to have
公司的決策程序。                                  expertise in relevant area to make contribution to the Company and
                                          to have sufficient time to participate in the decision making process of
                                          the Company.

連 任。馬 志 忠 先 生 已 與 本 公 司 訂 有 委 任 函，自       election in accordance with the Company’s articles association. Mr.
2023 年 3 月 11 日起為期兩年。                     Ma Zhizhong entered into the letter of appointment with the
                                          Company for a terms of two years commencing from 11 March 2023.

根據本公司組織章程細則（「章程細則」），當時三                   Pursuant to the Company’s articles of association (the “Articles”),
分之一董事，或倘董事人數並非三之倍數，則最                     one-third of the Directors for the time being (or if the number is not a
接近但不少於三分之一人數，須於各股東週年大                     multiple of three, then the number nearest to but not less than one-
會按照章程細則條文輪值退任。因此，全體董事                     third) shall retire from office by rotation at each AGM in accordance

主席與行政總裁的角色                                Roles of chairman and chief executive officer
守 則 第 二 部 分 第 C.2.1 條 規 定，主 席 與 行 政 總 裁   Code C.2.1 of Part 2 of the Code stipulates that the roles of chairman
的角色應有區分，且不應由同一人兼任。張建宏                     and chief executive officer should be separate and should not be
先生目前身兼本公司主席與行政總裁。董事會認                     performed by the same individual. Mr. Zhang Jianhong is both the
為，由一人同時兼任主席及行政總裁可令本集團                     Chairman and Chief Executive Officer of the Company. The Board
更能貫徹強勢領導，且在業務策劃上更具效率。                     believes that vesting the roles of both Chairman and Chief Executive
此外，董事會認為，在由多名經驗豐富且具才幹                     Officer in the same position provides the Group with stronger and
的人士所組成董事會密切監控下，此架構不會影                     more consistent leadership and allows for more effective planning.
響權力平衡。董事會對張先生充滿信心，並深信                     Further, the Board considers that this structure will not impair the
現時安排對本集團的業務前景最為有利。                        balance of power, which has been closely monitored by the Board,
                                          which comprises experienced and high caliber individuals. The Board
                                          has full confidence in Mr. Zhang and believes that the current
                                          arrangement is beneficial to the business prospect of the Group.

     異 將 用 作 決 定 董 事 會 的 最 佳 組 合。董 事 會        differences will be taken into account in determining the
     所 有 的 委 任 均 以 用 人 唯 才 為 原 則，並 於 考        optimum composition of the Board. All Board appointments will
     慮人選時以客觀條件充分顧及董事會成員                       be based on meritocracy, and candidates will be considered

然而，任何風險管理和內部監控系統旨在管理而                    However, risk management and internal control systems are designed
非消除未能達成業務目標的風險，而且只能就不                    to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failing to reach a business
會有重大的失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對的                    goal, and to make reasonable, not absolute, warranties of no
保證。                                      significant misrepresentation or loss.

以下列出公司對於有關處理及發放內幕消息的程                    Set out below is the Company’s procedures and internal controls for
序和內部監控措施：                                handling and distributing inside information:

•    公司明白其根據《證券及期貨條例》和上市                 •    The Company acknowledges its obligations under the Securities
     規 則 所 須 履 行 的 責 任，首 要 原 則 是 公 司 一        and Futures Ordinance and the Listing Rules. The first principle
     旦 知 悉 內 幕 消 息 及╱或 在 作 出 有 關 決 定 後        is that the information should be immediately published when
     須即時公佈，除非該等內幕消息屬於《證券                      the Company is aware of and/or when the related decision is

     治 (ESG) 管 理 流 程，構 建 一 套 完 善 的 ESG 管             management process and has established a set of
     理 框 架 以 促 進 業 務 的 可 持 續 發 展。同 時，                comprehensive ESG management framework to promote
     本 集 團 與 客 戶、消 費 者、員 工、投 資 者 和                   sustainable development of its business. Meanwhile, the Group

           為加強分子公司的環保自主管理能力，                                   In order to strengthen the independent management
           東岳集團建立環保管理制度考核機制。                                   ability of its subsidiaries in environmental protection,
           每月上旬，集團發佈月度專項檢查表，                                   Dongyue Group has established an assessment
           要求各分子公司根據檢查表內容進行                                    mechanism for environmental protection management
           自查。中旬，各分子公司需將自查發                                    system. At the beginning of each month, the Group
           現的問題及相應的整改計劃上報至集                                    publishes a monthly checklist and requires each subsidiary
           團環保處備案。對於已備案的問題，                                    to conduct self-inspection according to the content of the
           分子公司在集團的綜合檢查中將獲得                                    checklist. In the middle of the month, each subsidiary is
           考核豁免。下旬，集團將組織開展綜                                    required to report the problems found in the self-
           合檢查，對各分子公司的環保管理情                                    inspection together with the corresponding rectification
           況進行全面評估，並對按期完成整改                                    plan to the Group’s Environmental Protection Department
           的分子公司予以較高評分，以此激勵                                    for inspection. Problems filed by the subsidiaries will be
           分子公司積極落實環保整改措施，提                                    exempted from the Group’s comprehensive inspection. In
           升環保績效。                                              the late month, the Group organizes a comprehensive
                                                               inspection to assess the environmental management of
                                                               subsidiaries of the Group in an all-round manner and
                                                               gives higher scores to subsidiaries that have completed
                                                               the rectification work on schedule, with a view to

    血傳遞希望和溫暖。                      of blood without compensation, with a total of 29,600
                                   milliliters of blood donated, fully demonstrating the

                                   enterprise to convey hope and warmth.

INDEPENDENT AUDITOR’S REPORT




 致東岳集團有限公司全體股東                           To the Shareholders of Dongyue Group Limited
（於開曼群島註冊成立的有限公司）                         (incorporated in Cayman Islands with limited liability)

意見                                       Opinion
吾等已審計第 138 至 261 頁所載東岳集團有限公              We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Dongyue
司（「貴 公 司」）及其 附 屬公 司（統 稱「貴集 團」）的          Group Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (collectively
綜合財務報表，當中包括於 2023 年 12 月 31 日的           referred to as the “Group”) set out on pages 138 to 261, which
綜合財務狀況表及截至該日止年度的綜合損益及                    comprise the consolidated statement of financial position as at 31
其他全面收入報表、綜合權益變動表及綜合現金                    December 2023, and the consolidated statement of profit or loss and
流量表，以及綜合財務報表附註，包括重大會計                    other comprehensive income, the consolidated statement of changes
政策資料。                                    in equity and the consolidated statement of cash flows for the year
                                         then ended, and notes to the consolidated financial statements,
                                         including material accounting policy information.

吾等認為，綜合財務報表已遵照國際會計準則理                    In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and

附註：                                                    Notes:

(a)   股份溢價                                             (a)      Share premium

      根據開曼群島公司法（經修訂），本公司的股份溢價賬                                  Under the Companies Law (Revised) of the Cayman Islands, the share premium of
      可分配給本公司所有者，惟在緊接建議分派股息之日後，                                 the Company is distributable to the owners of the Company provided that
      本公司將有能力償還其在日常業務過程中到期的債務。                                  immediately following the date on which the dividend is proposed to be
                                                                distributed, the Company will be in a position to pay off its debts as they fall due
                                                                in the ordinary course of business.

(b)   按公平值計入其他全面收益的股本工具儲備                              (b)      Equity instruments at FVTOCI reserve

      按公平值計入其他全面收益的股本工具儲備包括於報告                                  The equity instruments at FVTOCI reserve comprises the cumulative net change in
      期末根據國際財務報告準則第 9 號持有之按公平值計入                                the fair value of equity instruments at FVTOCI under IFRS 9 that are held at the
      其他全面收益之股本工具之公平值累計淨變動。                                     end of the reporting period.

(c)   合併儲備                                             (c)      Merger reserve

      合併儲備來自於 2006 年完成之集團重組。                                    Merger reserve arose in group reorganisation completed in 2006.

(d)   股本儲備                                             (d)      Capital reserve

      於 2007 年 11 月 16 日，本公司購回所有早前已發行每股                         On 16 November 2007, the Company repurchased all of the 275,000,000
      面值 0.1 美元之普通股 275,000,000 股，而該等購回普通                       previously issued ordinary share of US$0.1 each and these repurchased ordinary
      股與截至該日期之所有法定但未發行股本一併註銷。同                                  shares were cancelled with all of the authorised but unissued share capital as of
      日，藉增設 4,000,000,000 股每股面值 0.1 港元之新普通                      that date. On the same date, the authorised share capital was increased to
      股，將法定股本增至 400,000,000 港元。於 2007 年 11 月                    HK$400,000,000 by the creation of 4,000,000,000 new ordinary shares of
      15 日，向 現 有 股 東 發 行 275,000,000 股 每 股 面 值 0.1 港            HK$0.1 each. 275,000,000 new ordinary shares of HK$0.1 each were then issued
      元之新普通股。購回金額超過已發行新股份面值之款額                                  to the shareholders existing on 15 November 2007. The excess of the repurchased
      直接計入股本儲備。                                                 amount over the nominal amount of new shares issued was credited directly to
                                                                the capital reserve.

      向附屬公司非控股股東收購額外權益確認為與非控股股                                  The acquisition of additional interest from non-controlling shareholders of
      東權益之交易，因此產生之相關折讓╱溢價直接於股本                                  subsidiaries were recognised as transactions with non-controlling interests and the
      儲備列賬╱支銷。                                                  corresponding discount/premium were credited/debited directly against capital
                                                                reserve.

(e)   安全儲備                                             (e)      Safety reserve

      根據中華人民共和國（「中國」）財政部及國家安全生產                                 Pursuant to certain regulations issued by the Ministry of Finance and the State
      監督管理總局頒佈的若干法規，本集團之若干中國附屬                                  Administration of Work Safety of the People’s Republic of China (“PRC”), some of
      公司須以累進比率按危險化學品銷售總收入之 0.5% 至                               the Group’s PRC subsidiaries are required to set aside an amount of safety reserve
      4% 預留一定數額之安全儲備金。該儲備可用作集團日                                 at progressive rates from 0.5% to 4% of the total revenue from the sales of
      常運作於安全生產改善及維修之用，被視為開支性質，                                  hazardous chemical. The reserve can be utilised for the spending in improvements
      並於產生時計入損益。                                                and maintenances of work safety on the Group’s daily operations, which are
                                                                considered expenses in nature and charged to the profit and loss as incurred.

(f)   法定盈餘儲備                                           (f)      Statutory surplus reserve

      根據中國公司法及相關公司組織章程細則，本公司中國                                  In accordance with the Company Law of PRC and the relevant Articles of
      附屬公司須將相等於根據中國會計準則釐定之除稅後溢                                  Association, the PRC subsidiaries of the Company are required to appropriate
      利 10% 之金額撥入法定盈餘儲備。                                        amount equal to 10% of their profit after taxation as determined in accordance
                                                                with the PRC accounting standards to the statutory surplus reserve.

      法定盈餘儲備為股東權益之一部分，當其結餘達到相當                                  Statutory surplus reserve is part of shareholders’ equity and when its balance
      於 註 冊 資 本 50% 之 金 額，則 毋 須 進 一 步 分 配。根 據 中                 reaches an amount equal to 50% of the registered capital, further appropriation is
      國公司法，法定盈餘儲備可用作填補過往虧損、增加生                                  not required. According to the Company Law of the PRC, statutory surplus reserve
      產及業務營運或轉增股本。                                              may be used to make up past losses, to increase production and business
                                                                operations or to increase capital by means of conversion.

      Grand Pavilion, Hibiscus Way, 802 West Bay       Box 31119, Grand Pavilion, Hibiscus Way, 802 West Bay Road,
      Road, Grand Cayman, KY1-1205, Cayman             Grand Cayman, KY1-1205, Cayman Islands. The shares of the
      Islands。本公司的股份自 2007 年 12 月 10 日                 Company have been listed on the Main Board of The Stock
      起在香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市。                               Exchange of Hong Kong Limited since 10 December 2007.

      本 公 司 為 一 間 投 資 控 股 公 司。本 公 司 及 其                The Company is an investment holding company. The Company
      附屬公司（合稱「本集團」）主要從事製造、                             and its subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the “Group”) are
      分 銷 及 銷 售 高 分 子 材 料、有 機 硅、製 冷 劑                  principally engaged in the manufacture, distribution and sale of
      及 二 氯 甲 烷、聚 氯 乙 烯（「PVC」）及 燒 鹼 以                  polymers, organic silicon, refrigerants and dichloromethane,

3.    重大會計政策概要                            3.   Summary of material accounting policies
      報告編製依據                                   Basis of preparation

      綜合財務報表乃根據國際會計準則理事會                       The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in
      頒 佈 的 國 際 財 務 報 告 準 則 編 製。另 外，綜          accordance with International Financial Reporting Standards
      合財務報表包括香港聯合交易所有限公司                       issued by International Accounting Standards Board. In addition,
      證券上市規則及香港公司條例規定之適用                       the consolidated financial statements include applicable
      披露。                                      disclosures required by the Rules Governing the Listing of
                                               Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited and by
                                               the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

      綜 合 財 務 報 表 乃 按 歷 史 成 本 基 準 編 製，惟        The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on
      金融工具於各報告期末乃按公平值計入損                       the historical cost basis except for financial instruments that are
      益 及 按 公 平 值 計 入 其 他 全 面 收 益 計 量。歷        measured at fair value through profit or loss and fair value
      史成本一般按交換貨品及服務之代價之公                       through other comprehensive income at the end of each
      平值計算。                                    reporting period. Historical cost is generally based on the fair
                                               value of the consideration given in exchange for goods and
                                               services.

      公平值指於計量日期市場參與者於有秩序                       Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or
      交易中出售一項資產可收取或轉讓一項負                       paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between
      債 須 支 付 之 價 格，不 論 該 價 格 是 否 直 接 可        market participants at the measurement date, regardless of
      觀 察 或 可 使 用 其 他 估 值 方 法 作 出 估 計。倘        whether that price is directly observable or estimated using
      市場參與者於計量日期對資產或負債定價                       another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value of an
      時 會 考 慮 資 產 或 負 債 的 特 點，則 本 集 團 於        asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the
      估計資產或負債的公平值時會考慮該等特                       characteristics of the asset or liability if market participants
      點。該 等 綜 合 財 務 報 表 中 作 計 量 及╱或 披          would take those characteristics into account when pricing the
      露 用 途 的 公 平 值 乃 按 此 基 準 釐 定，惟 屬 於        asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value for
      國際財務報告準則第2號以股份支付款項                       measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these consolidated
      範 圍 的 以 股 份 付 款 的 交 易、屬 於 國 際 財 務        financial statements is determined on such a basis, except for
      報告準則第 16 號範圍內的租賃交易，以及                    share-based payment transactions that are within the scope of
      與公平值有部分相若地方但並非公平值的                       IFRS 2 Share-based Payment, leasing transactions that are
      計量，譬如國際會計準則第 2 號存貨內的可                    accounted for in accordance with IFRS 16, and measurements
      變現淨值或國際會計準則第 36 號資產減值                    that have some similarities to fair value but are not fair value,
      的使用價值除外。                                 such as net realisable value in IAS 2 Inventories or value in use in
                                               IAS 36 Impairment of Assets.

      報告編製依據（續）                                Basis of preparation (Continued)

      此 外，出 於 財 務 報 告 目 的，公 平 值 計 量 應          In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value
      基於公平值計量的輸入值的可觀察程度以                       measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on the
      及該等輸入值對整體公平值計量的重要性，                      degree to which the inputs to the fair value measurements are
      被 歸 入 第 一 層、第 二 層 或 第 三 層 的 公 平 值        observable and the significance of the inputs to the fair value
      級次，詳述如下：                                 measurement in its entirety, which are described as follows:

      第一層輸入值是指於計量日期由相同資產                       Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets
      或負債在活躍市場中（未經調整）的報價得                      for identical assets or liabilities that the entity can access at the
      出；第 二 層 輸 入 值 是 指 由 除 了 第 一 層 輸 入        measurement date; Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted
      值 所 包 含 的 報 價 以 外 的，資 產 或 負 債 可 由        prices included within Level 1, that are observable for the asset
      直 接 或 間 接 觀 察 得 出 的 輸 入 值；及 第 三 層        or liability, either directly or indirectly; and Level 3 inputs are
      輸入值是指資產或負債的不可觀察輸入值。                      unobservable inputs for the asset or liability.

      重大會計政策載列如下。                              The material accounting policies are set out below.

      綜合基準                                     Basis of consolidation

      綜合財務報表包括本公司及由本公司及其                       The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial
      附 屬 公 司 控 制 的 實 體 的 財 務 報 表。當 本 公        statements of the Company and entities controlled by the
      司具備以下條件時，即具有控制權：                         Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the
                                               Company:

      —     有控制被投資方的權力；                        —     has power over the investee;

      —     可或有權自其與被投資方的關係中獲                   —      is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its
            得不同回報；及                                  involvement with the investee; and

      —     有能力運用其權力影響其回報。                     —     has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.

      倘事實和情況顯示上述的三項控制因素之                       The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if
      一 項 或 多 項 出 現 變 動，本 集 團 會 重 新 評 估        facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one
      其是否控制被投資方。                               or more of the three elements of control listed above.

3.    重大會計政策概要（續）                         3.   Summary of material accounting policies
                                               (Continued)

      綜合基準（續）                                  Basis of consolidation (Continued)

      附屬公司於本集團取得該附屬公司的控制                       Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains
      權 時 綜 合 入 賬，並 於 本 集 團 喪 失 對 該 附 屬        control over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses
      公司的控制權時終止綜合入賬。具體而言，                      control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income and expenses of a
      自本集團取得控制權當日起直至本集團不                       subsidiary acquired or disposed of during the year are included
      再 控 制 附 屬 公 司 當 日，於 年 內 收 購 或 出 售        in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other
      的附屬公司收支均計入綜合損益及其他全                       comprehensive income from the date the Group gains control
      面收入報表。                                   until the date when the Group ceases to control the subsidiary.

      損益及其他全面收益各項目歸屬至本公司                       Profit or loss and each item of other comprehensive income are
      擁 有 人 及 非 控 股 股 東 權 益。附 屬 公 司 的 全        attributed to the owners of the Company and to the non-
      面收益總額歸屬至本公司擁有人及非控股                       controlling interests. Total comprehensive income of subsidiaries
      股 東 權 益，即 使 此 舉 會 導 致 非 控 股 股 東 權        is attributed to the owners of the Company and to the non-
      益出現虧絀結餘。                                 controlling interests even if this results in the non-controlling
                                               interests having a deficit balance.

      附屬公司的財務報表於必要時會進行調整，                      When necessary, adjustments are made to the financial
      以使其會計政策與本集團的會計政策一致。                      statements of subsidiaries to bring their accounting policies into
                                               line with the Group’s accounting policies.

      與本集團成員間的交易有關的所有本集團                       All intragroup assets and liabilities, equity, income, expenses
      內 部 的 資 產 及 負 債、股 權、收 入、開 支 及            and cash flows relating to transactions between members of the
      現金流量均於綜合賬目時悉數對銷。                         Group are eliminated in full on consolidation.

      附屬公司業績及權益內的非控制性權益分                       Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of subsidiaries

3.    重大會計政策概要（續）                         3.   Summary of material accounting policies
                                               (Continued)

      本集團於現有附屬公司的權益變動                          Changes in the Group’s interests in existing subsidiaries

      倘本集團於附屬公司的權益變動並無導致                       Changes in the Group’s interests in subsidiaries that do not
      本 集 團 失 去 對 附 屬 公 司 的 控 制 權，則 入 賬        result in the Group losing control over the subsidiaries are
      列 作 股 本 交 易。本 集 團 相 關 權 益 及 非 控 股        accounted for as equity transactions. The carrying amounts of
      股東權益組成部分的賬面值已作出調整，                       the Group’s relevant components of equity and the non-
      以反映彼等於附屬公司的相對權益變動，                       controlling interests are adjusted to reflect the changes in their
      包括根據本集團與非控股股東權益的比例                       relative interests in the subsidiaries including re-attribution of

      客戶合約收益                                   Revenue from contracts with customers

      本 集 團 於 完 成 履 約 責 任 時，即 於 特 定 履 約        The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance
      責任相關的貨品或服務的「控制權」轉讓予                      obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or
      客戶時確認收益。                                 services underlying the particular performance obligation is
                                               transferred to the customer.

       履約責任指可明確區分的一項貨品或服務                      A performance obligation represents a good or service (or a
      （或 一組 貨品 或 服務），或 一 系列 大 致 相 同            bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of distinct
       的可明確區分的貨品或服務。                           goods or services that are substantially the same.

      倘 符 合 以 下 其 中 一 項 標 準，則 控 制 權 隨 時        Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised over
      間 轉 移，而 收 益 則 參 照 完 全 履 行 相 關 履 約        time by reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction
      責任的進展情況而隨時間確認：                           of the relevant performance obligation if one of the following
                                               criteria is met:

      —     客戶在本集團履約的同時即取得並消                   —     the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the
            耗本集團履約所帶來之利益；                            benefits provided by the Group’s performance as the
                                                     Group performs;

      —     本集團履約導致產生及提升一項資產，                  —     the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset
            而該項資產於產生或提升時由客戶控                         that the customer controls as the Group performs; or
            制；或

      —     本集團履約未產生對本集團具有替代                   —     the Group’s performance does not create an asset with an
            用途之資產，而本集團有執行權收取                         alternative use to the Group and the Group has an
            迄今已履約部分之款項。                              enforceable right to payment for performance completed
                                                     to date.

      否 則，收 益 於 客 戶 獲 得 特 定 貨 品 或 服 務 的        Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the
      控制權時確認。                                  customer obtains control of the distinct good or service.

      合約負債為本集團向客戶轉讓本集團已從                       A contract liability represents the Group’s obligation to transfer
      客戶收取代價（或應從客戶收取代價金額）                      goods or services to a customer for which the Group has

      借貸成本                                     Borrowing costs

      借 貸 成 本 直 接 源 自 收 購、建 造 或 生 產 合 資        Borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition,
      格 資 產，而 該 等 資 產 需 要 一 段 長 時 間 方 可        construction or production of qualifying assets, which are assets
      供 用 作 擬 定 用 途 或 出 售。借 貸 成 本 會 加 入        that necessarily take a substantial period of time to get ready
      作 該 等 資 產 的 成 本，直 至 資 產 大 致 上 可 供        for their intended use or sale, are added to the cost of those
      用作擬定用途或出售為止。                             assets until such time as the assets are substantially ready for
                                               their intended use or sale.

      於有關資產可供擬定用途或出售後仍未償                       Any specific borrowing that remains outstanding after the
      還的任何特定借貸計入一般借貸組合以計                       related asset is ready for its intended use or sale is included in
      算 一 般 借 款 的 資 本 化 率。尚 未 用 於 合 資 格        the general borrowing pool for calculation of capitalisation rate
      資產的特定借貸作暫時投資賺取的投資收                       on general borrowings. Investment income earned on the
      入，於合資格資本化的借貸成本中扣除。                       temporary investment of specific borrowings pending their
                                               expenditure on qualifying assets is deducted from the borrowing
                                               costs eligible for capitalisation.

      所有其他借貸成本均於產生期間在損益中                       All other borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss in the
      確認。                                      period in which they are incurred.

      政府補貼                                     Government grants

      在合理地保證本集團會遵守政府補貼的附                       Government grants are not recognised until there is reasonable
      帶 條 件，以 及 將 會 得 到 補 助 前，不 會 確 認          assurance that the Group will comply with the conditions
      政府補貼。                                    attaching to them and that the grants will be received.

      與 收 入 相 關 的 政 府 補 貼 乃 按 系 統 基 準，於        Government grants related to income are recognised in profit or
      本集團將有關補助擬補償的有關成本確認                       loss on a systematic basis over the periods in which the Group
      為 開 支 的 期 間 內，於 損 益 中 確 認。具 體 而          recognises as expenses the related costs for which the grants
      言，以 要 求 本 集 團 購 買、建 造 或 收 購 非 流          are intended to compensate. Specifically, government grants of
      動資產為主要條件的政府補貼乃於綜合財                       which the primary condition is that the Group should purchase,
      務 狀 況 表 中 確 認 為 遞 延 收 入，並 在 相 關 資        construct or otherwise acquire non-current assets are recognised
      產的可使用年期內按系統及合理基準轉撥                       as deferred income in the consolidated statement of financial
      至損益中。                                    position and transferred to profit or loss on a systematic and
                                               rational basis over the useful lives of the related assets.

      作為已產生的開支或虧損的補償或就給予                       Government grants related to income that are receivable as
      本 集 團 即 時 財 政 支 持 目 的，且 無 日 後 相 關        compensation for expenses or losses already incurred or for the
      成 本 的 應 收 與 收 入 相 關 的 政 府 補 貼，乃 於        purpose of giving immediate financial support to the Group
      其成為可收取的期間內於損益中確認。                        with no future related costs are recognised in profit or loss in

      稅項                                       Taxation

      所得稅開支指即期應付稅項及遞延稅項的                       Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently
      總和。                                      payable and deferred tax.

      即期應付稅項按本年度應課稅溢利計算。                       The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the year.
      應課稅溢利與綜合損益及其他全面收入報                       Taxable profit differs from profit before tax as reported in the
      表 中 呈 報 的 稅 前 溢 利 不 同，此 乃 由 於 其 他        consolidated statement of profit or loss and other
      年 度 的 應 課 稅 或 可 扣 稅 收 入 或 開 支，以 及        comprehensive income because income or expense that are
      從 未 課 稅 或 可 扣 稅 的 項 目 所 致。本 集 團 的        taxable or deductible in other years and items that are never
      即期稅項負債採用於報告期末已制定或實                       taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax is
      質制定的稅率計算。                                calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or
                                               substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.

      遞延稅項按綜合財務報表的資產及負債賬                       Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences between
      面值與用以計算應課稅溢利相應稅基的暫                       the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the consolidated
      時 差 額 確 認。遞 延 稅 項 負 債 通 常 會 就 所 有        financial statements and the corresponding tax base used in the
      應 課 稅 暫 時 差 額 確 認。遞 延 稅 項 資 產 一 般        computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are
      在很可能出現可利用暫時差額扣稅的應課                       generally recognised for all taxable temporary differences.
      稅 溢 利 時 就 所 有 可 扣 減 暫 時 差 額 確 認。若        Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for all deductible
      於 一 項 交 易 中，因 商 譽 或 因 業 務 合 併 以 外        temporary difference to the extent that it is probable that
      原因首次確認其他資產及負債而引致的暫                       taxable profits will be available against which those deductible
      時 差 額 既 不 影 響 應 課 稅 溢 利，亦 不 影 響 會        temporary differences can be utilised. Such assets and liabilities
      計溢利，則不會確認該等資產及負債。                        are not recognised if the temporary difference arises from
                                               goodwill or from the initial recognition (other than in a business
                                               combination) of other assets and liabilities in a transaction that
                                               affects neither the taxable profit nor the accounting profit.

      遞延稅項負債乃按因於附屬公司及聯營公                       Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary
      司的投資而引致的應課稅暫時差額而確認，                      differences associated with investments in subsidiaries and
      惟若本集團可控制暫時差額的撥回及暫時                       associates, except where the Group is able to control the
      差額很有可能不會於可見將來撥回的情況                       reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable that the
      除 外。因 與 有 關 投 資 及 權 益 相 關 的 可 扣 減        temporary difference will not reverse in the foreseeable future.
      暫時差額而產生的遞延稅項資產僅在可能                       Deferred tax assets arising from deductible temporary
      產生足夠應課稅溢利以動用暫時差額利益                       differences associated with such investments and interests are
      並預期可在可見將來撥回時確認。                          only recognised to the extent that it is probable that there will
                                               be sufficient taxable profits against which to utilise the benefits
                                               of the temporary differences and they are expected to reverse in
                                               the foreseeable future.

      收 益 或 虧 損，其 將 於 出 售 相 關 資 產 或 負 債        of the related assets or liabilities. Therefore, if a gain or loss
      時 重 新 分 類 至 損 益，本 集 團 於 出 售╱部 分          previously recognised in other comprehensive income by that
      出售有關聯營公司時將收益或虧損由權益                       associate would be reclassified to profit or loss on the disposal
      重新分類至損益（作為重新分類調整）。                       of the related assets or liabilities, the Group reclassifies the gain
                                               or loss from equity to profit or loss (as a reclassification
                                               adjustment) upon disposal/partial disposal of the relevant
                                               associate.

      當本集團減少其於聯營公司的所有權權益                       When the Group reduces its ownership interest in an associate
      但 本 集 團 繼 續 使 用 權 益 法 時，倘 有 關 收 益        but the Group continues to use the equity method, the Group
      或虧損將於出售相關資產或負債時重新分                       reclassified to profit or loss the proportion of the gain or loss
      類 至 損 益，則 本 集 團 將 先 前 已 於 其 他 全 面        that had previously been recognised in other comprehensive
      收益確認的與該所有權權益減少有關的收                       income relating to that reduction in ownership interest if that
      益 或 虧 損 部 分 重 新 分 類 至 損 益。倘 集 團 實        gain or loss would be reclassified to profit or loss on the disposal
      體 與 本 集 團 聯 營 公 司 進 行 交 易，與 聯 營 公        of the related assets or liabilities. When a group entity transacts
      司或合資企業進行交易所產生的溢利及虧                       with an associate of the Group, profits and losses resulting from
      損 於 綜 合 財 務 報 表 內 確 認，惟 僅 以 與 本 集        the transactions with the associate or joint venture are
      團無關的聯營公司或合資企業權益為限。                       recognised in the consolidated financial statements only to the
                                               extent of interests in the associate or joint venture that are not
                                               related to the Group.

      倘屬以下情況，金融資產被分類為持作買賣：                     A financial asset is held for trading if:

      根據國際財務報告準則第9號進行減值評                       Impairment of financial assets subject to impairment assessment
      估的金融資產減值                                 under IFRS 9
      本集團根據國際財務報告準則第9號須予                       The Group performs impairment assessment under expected
      減 值 的 金 融 資 產（包 括 貿 易 及 其 他 應 收 款        credit loss (“ECL”) model on financial assets (including trade
      項、已抵押銀行存款以及銀行結餘及現金）                      and other receivables, pledged bank deposits, and bank
      按預期信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）模式進                     balances and cash) which are subject to impairment under IFRS
      行 減 值 評 估。預 期 信 貸 虧 損 的 金 額 於 各 報        9. The amount of ECL is updated at each reporting date to
      告 日 期 更 新，以 反 映 信 貸 風 險 自 初 步 確 認        reflect changes in credit risk since initial recognition.
      以來的變動。

      存續期預期信貸虧損指於相關工具之預計                       Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from all possible
      年期內所有可能發生之違約事件所將產生                       default events over the expected life of the relevant instrument.
      之 預 期 信 貸 虧 損。反 之，12 個 月 預 期 信 貸         In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m ECL”) represents the portion
      虧損（「12 個月預期信貸虧損」）指預期於報                   of lifetime ECL that is expected to result from default events
      告日期後 12 個月內可能發生之違約事件所                    that are possible within 12 months after the reporting date.
      將 產 生 之 部 分 存 續 期 預 期 信 貸 虧 損。評 估        Assessments are done based on the Group’s historical credit loss
      乃 按 本 集 團 過 往 信 貸 虧 損 經 驗 進 行，並 就        experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors,
      與 債 務 人 有 關 之 特 定 因 素、整 體 經 濟 情 況        general economic conditions and an assessment of both the
      以及對於報告日期之當前情況及預測之未                       current conditions at the reporting date as well as the forecast
      來情況兩者所作之評估作出調整。                          of future conditions.

      本集團一直就貿易應收款項確認存續期預                       The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for trade receivables.
      期信貸虧損。

      就 所 有 其 他 工 具 而 言，本 集 團 計 量 之 虧 損        For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss
      撥備相等於 12 個月預期信貸虧損，除非自                    allowance equal to 12m ECL, unless there has been a significant
      初 步 確 認 起 信 貸 風 險 顯 著 增 加，在 此 情 況        increase in credit risk since initial recognition, in which case the
      下 本 集 團 將 確 認 存 續 期 預 期 信 貸 虧 損。對        Group recognises lifetime ECL. The assessment of whether
      於 應 否 確 認 存 續 期 預 期 信 貸 虧 損，有 關 評        lifetime ECL should be recognised is based on significant
      估乃基於自初步確認起發生違約之可能性                       increases in the likelihood or risk of a default occurring since
      或風險有否大幅增加。                               initial recognition.

            性。                                  similar credit risk characteristics.

     借貸）其後採用實際利息法按攤銷成本計量。                      borrowings) are subsequently measured at amortised cost, using
                                               the effective interest method.

      實際利息法是一種計算金融負債的攤銷成                       The effective interest method is a method of calculating the
      本 及 於 有 關 期 間 攤 分 利 息 開 支 的 方 法。實        amortised cost of a financial liability and of allocating interest
      際利率是於初步確認時準確地透過金融負                       expense over the relevant period. The effective interest rate is
      債預計年期或（如適用）於較短期間內將估                      the rate that exactly discounts estimated future cash payments
      計日後現金付款貼現為賬面淨值的利率。                       through the expected life of the financial liability, or where
                                               appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount on
                                               initial recognition.

      於應用附註 3 所述的本集團會計政策時，本                    In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which are
      公司董事須就並不容易從其他來源顯示的                       described in note 3, the directors of the Company are required

      估計不同。                                    historical experience and other factors that are considered to be
                                               relevant. Actual results may differ from these estimates.

      估 計 及 相 關 假 設 會 持 續 檢 討。倘 會 計 估 計        The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an
      的 修 訂 僅 對 估 計 修 訂 的 期 間 構 成 影 響，則        ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised
      有 關 修 訂 於 該 期 間 確 認，或 倘 有 關 修 訂 對        in the period in which the estimate is revised if the revision
      本 期 間 及 未 來 期 間 均 構 成 影 響，則 修 訂 會        affects only that period, or in the period of the revision and
      於該修訂期間及未來期間確認。                           future periods if the revision affects both current and future

      6.2   經營分部                                 6.2   Operating Segments

            本集團業務乃按產品的類型劃分。向                           The Group’s operations are organized based on the
            本公司董事會（即總營運決策人）（「總                         different types of products. Information reported to the
            營運決策人」）報告以作資源分配及分                          board of directors of the Company, being the chief
            部表現評估的資料亦按產品及物業開                           operating decision maker (“CODM”), for the purposes of
            發的類型呈列。此乃本集團之構造基                           resource allocation and assessment of segment
            礎。                                         performance focuses on types of products and property
                                                       development. This is the basis upon which the Group is
                                                       organized.

            本集團之經營及呈報分部如下：                             The Group’s operating and reportable segments are as
                                                       follows:

            —     高分子材料；                               —    Polymers;

      額乃根據估計未來現金流量貼現至其現值                        such valuation. The amount is determined based on the cash
      的 現 金 流 量 預 測 釐 定，並 需 要 使 用 主 要 假         flow projection for the estimated future cash flow discounted to
      設，包 括 貼 現 率、最 終 增 長 率、預 算 收 益             its present value and requires the use of key assumptions,
      及 毛 利 率，並 考 慮 到 相 關 行 業 增 長 預 測 及         including the discount rate, terminal growth rate, budgeted
      管理層就對市場發展的期望及市場方法，                        revenue and gross margin taking into account the relevant

      在 承 接 任 何 新 客 戶 前，本 集 團 會 調 查 新 客               Before accepting any new customer, the Group conducts
      戶的信譽及評估潛在客戶的信貸質素和確                              research on the creditworthiness of the new customer and
      定 客 戶 的 信 用 額 度。客 戶 的 信 用 額 度 和 信               assesses the potential customer’s credit quality and defines
      用 評 級 每 年 複 核 兩 次。99%（ 2022 年：                  credit limits by customer. Limits and scoring attributed to
      99%）未逾期亦未減值之貿易應收款項及應                            customers are reviewed twice a year. 99% (2022: 99%) of the
      收 票 據 擁 有 信 譽 調 查 的 良 好 信 用 評 級。本               trade and bills receivables that are neither past due nor impaired
      集 團 視 乎 客 戶 合 約 金 額、信 用 及 聲 譽 提 供               have the high-ranking record attributable under the research on
      多種還款期。                                          the creditworthiness. The Group offers various settlement terms

      本集團在中國的僱員乃中國各個地方政府                       The employees of the Group in the PRC are members of stage-
      營 運 的 國 家 退 休 福 利 計 劃 的 成 員。本 集 團        managed retirement benefit schemes operated by the respective
      須 向 計 劃 供 款 指 定 百 分 比（按 薪 酬 成 本 計        local governments in the PRC. The Group is required to
      算），作為有關福利的資金。本集團的唯一                      contribute a specified percentage of payroll costs to the
      責任是對該等計劃作出指定供款。                          schemes to fund the benefits. The only obligation of the Group
                                               with respect to these schemes is to make the specified
                                               contributions.
